22a1(1): @#/ O /po gangs kyi tshogs su byas //chu'i klung bskyed tha ba dgon gdung yul du 'gyur //de bzhin mthu chen bskyed med kha dang ser 'bab ste //nga rgyal snyems

23a1(1): @#/ O /su dag /khyod kyi yon tan tshogs chen mnga' ba de dag la //mthun pa'i tshul lus dran te dper blang ston snyam na //mi mnyam pa dpe las 'das p+sa

28b6(1): mtshan tshod 'dzin dbang yang bsgyur //'og phyogs brtan zhing spyi bo mnyen 'jam dga' bde ba //lha sde mang por 'du zhing dgyes par gnas gyi gzhi //de bzhin khyod kyi dbang

38a1(1): @#/ O /gtogs pa'i yon tan bstod pas na //'khro bo nyan thos dge 'dun kun kyang bstod par gyur //skyes bu seng ge khyod dang bcas la bstod pa y+wi//dkon mchog
